{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CgMqahFMuyLFxJGy881cL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"=== BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: CsvStat\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Module layout, package boundaries, and technical decisions for the CsvStat command-line CSV profiler. |\n| Depends On  | None |\n| Provides    | go.mod, cmd/csvstat, internal/cli Run |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nCsvStat is a single-binary Go command-line tool. It reads one CSV file, infers a type per column,\nand writes either a profile CSV or a frequency CSV to standard output. Failures write nothing to\nstandard output, one diagnostic line to standard error, and exit with a distinct code.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Go 1.22 or newer, standard library only | Every package; no third-party module in `go.mod` |\n| go test and go vet | Automated tests and `bin/test.sh` |\n| CSV (`encoding/csv`) | Reading the input file and writing all standard output |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader |\n\n## Module Layout\n\n```text\ngo.mod                    module csvstat, go 1.22 or newer, no require lines\ncmd/csvstat/main.go       package main: calls cli.Run(os.Args[1:], os.Stdout, os.Stderr) and os.Exit\ninternal/cli              Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int; argument parsing, exit codes\ninternal/reader           Read, IsNull, ErrRejected; file reading, trimming, validation, null rule\ninternal/infer            Infer; column type inference\ninternal/stats            counts, min, max, sum, mean, median using math/big\ninternal/format           FormatDecimal; two-decimal half-away-from-zero formatting\ninternal/output           profile and frequency CSV writers\nbin/test.sh               POSIX sh: go vet ./... then go test ./...\n```\n\nOnly `main` calls `os.Exit`. `cli.Run` uses a local `flag.FlagSet` with `flag.ContinueOnError` and\ndiscarded flag output, and is tested with `bytes.Buffer`.\n\n## Data Flow\n\n1. `cli.Run` parses and validates arguments (usage errors exit 2 before the file is opened).\n2. `reader.Read` opens the file; failure to open or read exits 1.\n3. `reader.Read` parses with `encoding/csv`, trims, and validates; rejection exits 3.\n4. `infer.Infer` types each column from its non-null values.\n5. `stats` computes exact values; `format` rounds once when writing.\n6. `output` writes CSV with `\\n` line endings to standard output; exit 0.\n\nOutput is assembled in memory and written only after every step succeeds, so a failing command\nnever writes to standard output.\n\n## Exit Codes\n\n| Code | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| 0 | Success |\n| 1 | File cannot be opened or read |\n| 2 | Usage error |\n| 3 | Rejected input: empty file, empty or repeated header name, ragged record, unknown column name |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Sums, means, and medians use `math/big` rationals and are rounded once, half away from zero.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` or any supplied data file and embeds no statistic.\n- Diagnostics go to standard error and are never compared.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Low-level APIs allowed |\n|---|---|---|\n| Input file access | `internal/reader` | `os.Open`, `encoding/csv` reader |\n| Standard output | `internal/output` | `encoding/csv` writer |\n| Process exit | `cmd/csvstat` | `os.Exit` |\n| Persistence, configuration, file store, external services | None; the tool has none | None |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-module-builds ===\nIntent: The application root is a Go module whose ./cmd/csvstat package builds with go build.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as work:\n    target = os.path.join(work, \"csvstat\")\n    result = subprocess.run([\"go\", \"build\", \"-o\", target, \"./cmd/csvstat\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    print(result.stdout)\n    print(result.stderr)\n    assert result.returncode == 0\n    assert os.path.isfile(target)\n=== END AC arch-module-builds ===\n\n=== AC arch-stdlib-only ===\nIntent: go.mod declares a Go version of 1.22 or newer and no third-party requirement.\n\nimport re\n\nwith open(\"go.mod\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    text = handle.read()\nversion = re.search(r\"^go\\s+(\\d+)\\.(\\d+)\", text, re.MULTILINE)\nassert version is not None\nassert (int(version.group(1)), int(version.group(2))) >= (1, 22)\nassert re.search(r\"^\\s*require\\b\", text, re.MULTILINE) is None\nassert re.search(r\"^\\s*replace\\b\", text, re.MULTILINE) is None\n=== END AC arch-stdlib-only ===\n\n=== AC arch-vet-clean ===\nIntent: go vet reports no problem across the module.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\n\nresult = subprocess.run([\"go\", \"vet\", \"./...\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\n=== END AC arch-vet-clean ===\n\n=== AC arch-layout ===\nIntent: The main package and the internal packages named by the architecture exist, and main delegates to cli.Run.\n\nimport os\n\nassert os.path.isfile(os.path.join(\"cmd\", \"csvstat\", \"main.go\"))\nassert os.path.isdir(os.path.join(\"internal\", \"cli\"))\nwith open(os.path.join(\"cmd\", \"csvstat\", \"main.go\"), encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    main_source = handle.read()\nassert \"cli.Run\" in main_source\nassert \"os.Exit\" in main_source\n=== END AC arch-layout ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no third-party module and no vendored toolchain.\n- Do not modify, rewrite, regenerate, or substitute any file under `sources/`.\n- Do not read `sources/run.sh` or any supplied data file from application code, and do not hardcode a statistic, type, or value list.\n- Only `cmd/csvstat` calls `os.Exit`.\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliArguments.md ===\n# FEATURE: Command Line Arguments\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Parse the csvstat options that precede exactly one file argument and reject malformed argument lists with exit 2. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | --column, --freq, --top parsing |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`cli.Run` accepts these forms:\n\n```text\ncsvstat [--column <name>] <file>\ncsvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>\n```\n\nOptions precede the file and exactly one file is required. Parsing uses a local `flag.FlagSet`\nwith `flag.ContinueOnError` and discarded flag output. Arguments are validated before the file is\nopened, so a usage error never depends on the file's existence.\n\n## Behavior\n\n| Input | Result |\n|---|---|\n| No arguments | exit 2 |\n| Two or more positional arguments | exit 2 |\n| Unknown option | exit 2 |\n| Option with no value (`--column`, `--freq`, `--top` as last argument) | exit 2 |\n| `--top` not a positive integer (`0`, negative, non-numeric) | exit 2 |\n| `--top` without `--freq` | exit 2 |\n| `--column` together with `--freq` | exit 2 |\n| Option placed after the file | the option is an extra positional argument: exit 2 (decision Q-001) |\n\nEvery usage error writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error.\n`--top` defaults to 5 when `--freq` is given alone. The parsed result carries the column name, the\nfrequency column, the top count, and the file path for the command stories that follow.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-no-arguments ===\nIntent: Running with no arguments is a usage error with empty standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner()\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 2\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC cli-no-arguments ===\n\n=== AC cli-extra-file ===\nIntent: Two file arguments are a usage error with empty standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as work:\n    first = os.path.join(work, \"first.csv\")\n    second = os.path.join(work, \"second.csv\")\n    for path in (first, second):\n        with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\") as handle:\n            handle.write(\"a,b\\n1,2\\n\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(first, second)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 2\n    assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC cli-extra-file ===\n\n=== AC cli-unknown-option ===\nIntent: An unknown option is a usage error even when the file exists.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; The file is UTF-8 CSV as RFC 4180 defines it, read with `encoding/csv`. The first record is the header.\n2. The reader sets `FieldsPerRecord = -1` so a ragged record is detected by this package and reported with its line number.\n3. Every header name and every value is trimmed of leading and trailing spaces before any other rule applies.\n4. The file is rejected when it is empty, when a header name is empty after trimming, when a header name repeats, or when any record has a different field count from the header.\n5. A CSV syntax error from `encoding/csv`, such as a bare quote, is also a rejection (decision Q-003).\n6. A header with no data records is valid and yields zero rows.\n7. A rejection returns an error that satisfies `errors.Is(err, reader.ErrRejected)` and carries the file name and, for a record-level rejection, the record's line number. The `cli` package maps it to exit 3.\n8. A file that cannot be opened or read returns an error that is not `ErrRejected`; the `cli` package maps it to exit 1.\n9. Quoted fields may contain commas, quotes, and newlines.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in `internal/reader`, each writing its own file under `t.TempDir()`:\n\n| Test | Proves |\n|---|---|\n| `TestReadTrimsHeaderAndValues` | padded header names and values are trimmed |\n| `TestReadQuotedComma` | a quoted field containing a comma is one value |\n| `TestReadHeaderOnly` | a header with no records yields zero rows |\n| `TestReadRejectsEmptyFile` | a zero-byte file is `ErrRejected` |\n| `TestReadRejectsEmptyHeaderName` | an empty header name is `ErrRejected` |\n| `TestReadRejectsRepeatedHeaderName` | a repeated header name is `ErrRejected` |\n| `TestReadRejectsRaggedRecord` | a record with an extra field is `ErrRejected` and its message names the line number |\n| `TestReadMissingFileIsNotRejected` | an unopenable file is an error that is not `ErrRejected` |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC reading-trims ===\nIntent: The reader trims header names and values.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadTrimsHeaderAndValues\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-trims ===\n\n=== AC reading-quoted-comma ===\nIntent: A quoted field containing a comma is read as a single value.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadQuotedComma\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-quoted-comma ===\n\n=== AC reading-header-only ===\nIntent: A header with no data records is a valid table with zero rows.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadHeaderOnly\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-header-only ===\n\n=== AC reading-rejects-empty ===\nIntent: An empty file is rejected.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadRejectsEmptyFile\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-rejects-empty ===\n\n=== AC reading-rejects-empty-header ===\nIntent: An empty header name is rejected.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; Values are already trimmed by the\nreader. All other statistics treat null values as absent from type inference, distinct counts,\nmin, max, sum, mean, median, and frequency output, while they still count toward `rows` and `nulls`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Empty string is null.\n- `NA`, `N/A`, and `NULL`, compared ignoring case (`na`, `n/a`, `Null`), are null.\n- Every other value is non-null, including `NAN`, `none`, `0`, `-`, and `N/A ` with interior differences such as `N / A`.\n- A value that was only spaces becomes empty after trimming and is therefore null.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nWritten in `internal/reader`:\n\n| Test | Proves |\n|---|---|\n| `TestIsNullEmpty` | the empty string is null |\n| `TestIsNullTokensIgnoreCase` | `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL` in upper, lower, and mixed case are null |\n| `TestIsNullRejectsOtherValues` | `NAN`, `none`, `0`, `-`, `nullx`, and `N / A` are non-null |\n| `TestReadSpacesOnlyValueIsNull` | a value of only spaces, read through `Read`, is null |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC nulls-empty ===\nIntent: The empty string is null.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; A column's type is the first matching type, in order, that every non-null\nvalue satisfies. This story implements `integer` and `number`; later stories add `date`, `boolean`,\n`text`, and `empty` to the same function. Until they land, a column matching neither numeric type\nreturns `text`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `integer`: every non-null value matches `-?[0-9]+`.\n- `number`: every non-null value matches `-?[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?`.\n- `integer` is tested before `number`, so a column of whole numbers is `integer` and one containing any decimal value is `number`.\n- Null values (per `IsNull`) are skipped.\n- No leading `+`, no exponent, no `1.`, no `.5`, no thousands separator, and no surrounding text match either type.\n- A column mixing numeric and non-numeric values does not match.\n- Examples from the supplied data: a column of ids is integer, a column of prices with two decimals is number, a column containing `A7` and `12.5` is not numeric.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nWritten in `internal/infer`:\n\n| Test | Proves |\n|---|---|\n| `TestInferInteger` | whole numbers including negatives are integer |\n| `TestInferNumberWhenAnyDecimal` | one decimal value among whole numbers makes number |\n| `TestIntegerWinsOverNumber` | a column satisfying both is integer |\n| `TestInferSkipsNulls` | null values do not prevent a numeric type |\n| `TestInferRejectsMalformedNumbers` | `1.`, `.5`, `+1`, `1e3`, `1,5`, and `12abc` are neither integer nor number |\n| `TestInferMixedIsNotNumeric` | a column with a numeric and a non-numeric value is neither type |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC numeric-integer ===\nIntent: Columns of whole numbers, negatives included, are typed integer.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: CsvStat\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Module layout, package boundaries, and technical decisions for the CsvStat command-line CSV profiler. |\n| Depends On  | None |\n| Provides    | go.mod, cmd/csvstat, internal/cli Run |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nCsvStat is a single-binary Go command-line tool. It reads one CSV file, infers a type per column,\nand writes either a profile CSV or a frequency CSV to standard output. Failures write nothing to\nstandard output, one diagnostic line to standard error, and exit with a distinct code.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Go 1.22 or newer, standard library only | Every package; no third-party module in `go.mod` |\n| go test and go vet | Automated tests and `bin/test.sh` |\n| CSV (`encoding/csv`) | Reading the input file and writing all standard output |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader |\n\n## Module Layout\n\n```text\ngo.mod                    module csvstat, go 1.22 or newer, no require lines\ncmd/csvstat/main.go       package main: calls cli.Run(os.Args[1:], os.Stdout, os.Stderr) and os.Exit\ninternal/cli              Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int; argument parsing, exit codes\ninternal/reader           Read, IsNull, ErrRejected; file reading, trimming, validation, null rule\ninternal/infer            Infer; column type inference\ninternal/stats            counts, min, max, sum, mean, median using math/big\ninternal/format           FormatDecimal; two-decimal half-away-from-zero formatting\ninternal/output           profile and frequency CSV writers\nbin/test.sh               POSIX sh: go vet ./... then go test ./...\n```\n\nOnly `main` calls `os.Exit`. `cli.Run` uses a local `flag.FlagSet` with `flag.ContinueOnError` and\ndiscarded flag output, and is tested with `bytes.Buffer`.\n\n## Data Flow\n\n1. `cli.Run` parses and validates arguments (usage errors exit 2 before the file is opened).\n2. `reader.Read` opens the file; failure to open or read exits 1.\n3. `reader.Read` parses with `encoding/csv`, trims, and validates; rejection exits 3.\n4. `infer.Infer` types each column from its non-null values.\n5. `stats` computes exact values; `format` rounds once when writing.\n6. `output` writes CSV with `\\n` line endings to standard output; exit 0.\n\nOutput is assembled in memory and written only after every step succeeds, so a failing command\nnever writes to standard output.\n\n## Exit Codes\n\n| Code | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| 0 | Success |\n| 1 | File cannot be opened or read |\n| 2 | Usage error |\n| 3 | Rejected input: empty file, empty or repeated header name, ragged record, unknown column name |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Sums, means, and medians use `math/big` rationals and are rounded once, half away from zero.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` or any supplied data file and embeds no statistic.\n- Diagnostics go to standard error and are never compared.\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Low-level APIs allowed |\n|---|---|---|\n| Input file access | `internal/reader` | `os.Open`, `encoding/csv` reader |\n| Standard output | `internal/output` | `encoding/csv` writer |\n| Process exit | `cmd/csvstat` | `os.Exit` |\n| Persistence, configuration, file store, external services | None; the tool has none | None |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-module-builds ===\nIntent: The application root is a Go module whose ./cmd/csvstat package builds with go build.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as work:\n    target = os.path.join(work, \"csvstat\")\n    result = subprocess.run([\"go\", \"build\", \"-o\", target, \"./cmd/csvstat\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\n    print(result.stdout)\n    print(result.stderr)\n    assert result.returncode == 0\n    assert os.path.isfile(target)\n=== END AC arch-module-builds ===\n\n=== AC arch-stdlib-only ===\nIntent: go.mod declares a Go version of 1.22 or newer and no third-party requirement.\n\nimport re\n\nwith open(\"go.mod\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    text = handle.read()\nversion = re.search(r\"^go\\s+(\\d+)\\.(\\d+)\", text, re.MULTILINE)\nassert version is not None\nassert (int(version.group(1)), int(version.group(2))) >= (1, 22)\nassert re.search(r\"^\\s*require\\b\", text, re.MULTILINE) is None\nassert re.search(r\"^\\s*replace\\b\", text, re.MULTILINE) is None\n=== END AC arch-stdlib-only ===\n\n=== AC arch-vet-clean ===\nIntent: go vet reports no problem across the module.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport subprocess\n\nresult = subprocess.run([\"go\", \"vet\", \"./...\"], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.returncode == 0\n=== END AC arch-vet-clean ===\n\n=== AC arch-layout ===\nIntent: The main package and the internal packages named by the architecture exist, and main delegates to cli.Run.\n\nimport os\n\nassert os.path.isfile(os.path.join(\"cmd\", \"csvstat\", \"main.go\"))\nassert os.path.isdir(os.path.join(\"internal\", \"cli\"))\nwith open(os.path.join(\"cmd\", \"csvstat\", \"main.go\"), encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    main_source = handle.read()\nassert \"cli.Run\" in main_source\nassert \"os.Exit\" in main_source\n=== END AC arch-layout ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no third-party module and no vendored toolchain.\n- Do not modify, rewrite, regenerate, or substitute any file under `sources/`.\n- Do not read `sources/run.sh` or any supplied data file from application code, and do not hardcode a statistic, type, or value list.\n- Only `cmd/csvstat` calls `os.Exit`.\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliArguments.md ===\n# FEATURE: Command Line Arguments\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Parse the csvstat options that precede exactly one file argument and reject malformed argument lists with exit 2. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | --column, --freq, --top parsing |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`cli.Run` accepts these forms:\n\n```text\ncsvstat [--column <name>] <file>\ncsvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>\n```\n\nOptions precede the file and exactly one file is required. The file is UTF-8 CSV as RFC 4180 defines it, read with `encoding/csv`. The first record is the header.\n2. The reader sets `FieldsPerRecord = -1` so a ragged record is detected by this package and reported with its line number.\n3. Every header name and every value is trimmed of leading and trailing spaces before any other rule applies.\n4. The file is rejected when it is empty, when a header name is empty after trimming, when a header name repeats, or when any record has a different field count from the header.\n5. A CSV syntax error from `encoding/csv`, such as a bare quote, is also a rejection (decision Q-003).\n6. A header with no data records is valid and yields zero rows.\n7. A rejection returns an error that satisfies `errors.Is(err, reader.ErrRejected)` and carries the file name and, for a record-level rejection, the record's line number. The `cli` package maps it to exit 3.\n8. A file that cannot be opened or read returns an error that is not `ErrRejected`; the `cli` package maps it to exit 1.\n9. Quoted fields may contain commas, quotes, and newlines.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in `internal/reader`, each writing its own file under `t.TempDir()`:\n\n| Test | Proves |\n|---|---|\n| `TestReadTrimsHeaderAndValues` | padded header names and values are trimmed |\n| `TestReadQuotedComma` | a quoted field containing a comma is one value |\n| `TestReadHeaderOnly` | a header with no records yields zero rows |\n| `TestReadRejectsEmptyFile` | a zero-byte file is `ErrRejected` |\n| `TestReadRejectsEmptyHeaderName` | an empty header name is `ErrRejected` |\n| `TestReadRejectsRepeatedHeaderName` | a repeated header name is `ErrRejected` |\n| `TestReadRejectsRaggedRecord` | a record with an extra field is `ErrRejected` and its message names the line number |\n| `TestReadMissingFileIsNotRejected` | an unopenable file is an error that is not `ErrRejected` |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC reading-trims ===\nIntent: The reader trims header names and values.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadTrimsHeaderAndValues\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-trims ===\n\n=== AC reading-quoted-comma ===\nIntent: A quoted field containing a comma is read as a single value.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadQuotedComma\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-quoted-comma ===\n\n=== AC reading-header-only ===\nIntent: A header with no data records is a valid table with zero rows.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadHeaderOnly\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-header-only ===\n\n=== AC reading-rejects-empty ===\nIntent: An empty file is rejected.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadRejectsEmptyFile\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-rejects-empty ===\n\n=== AC reading-rejects-empty-header ===\nIntent: An empty header name is rejected.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadRejectsEmptyHeaderName\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-rejects-empty-header ===\n\n=== AC reading-rejects-repeated-header ===\nIntent: A repeated header name is rejected.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadRejectsRaggedRecord\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC reading-rejects-ragged ===\n\n=== AC reading-missing-file ===\nIntent: An unopenable file yields an error distinct from a rejection.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; no hand-rolled CSV parser.\n- Trimming applies to header names and values before every other rule.\n- Rejection errors name the file; record-level rejections also name the line number. Diagnostics go to standard error only.\n- The reader never writes to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-NullValues.md ===\n# FEATURE: Null Values\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Classify a trimmed value as null when it is empty or equals NA, N/A, or NULL ignoring case. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CsvReading.md |\n| Provides    | IsNull |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nPackage `internal/reader` exports `IsNull(value string) bool`. Values are already trimmed by the\nreader. All other statistics treat null values as absent from type inference, distinct counts,\nmin, max, sum, mean, median, and frequency output, while they still count toward `rows` and `nulls`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Empty string is null.\n- `NA`, `N/A`, and `NULL`, compared ignoring case (`na`, `n/a`, `Null`), are null.\n- Every other value is non-null, including `NAN`, `none`, `0`, `-`, and `N/A ` with interior differences such as `N / A`.\n- A value that was only spaces becomes empty after trimming and is therefore null.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nWritten in `internal/reader`:\n\n| Test | Proves |\n|---|---|\n| `TestIsNullEmpty` | the empty string is null |\n| `TestIsNullTokensIgnoreCase` | `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL` in upper, lower, and mixed case are null |\n| `TestIsNullRejectsOtherValues` | `NAN`, `none`, `0`, `-`, `nullx`, and `N / A` are non-null |\n| `TestReadSpacesOnlyValueIsNull` | a value of only spaces, read through `Read`, is null |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC nulls-empty ===\nIntent: The empty string is null.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestIsNullEmpty\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nulls-empty ===\n\n=== AC nulls-tokens ===\nIntent: NA, N/A, and NULL are null in any letter case.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestIsNullTokensIgnoreCase\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nulls-tokens ===\n\n=== AC nulls-other-values ===\nIntent: Values that merely resemble null tokens are non-null.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestIsNullRejectsOtherValues\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nulls-other-values ===\n\n=== AC nulls-spaces-only ===\nIntent: A value of only spaces is trimmed to empty and classified as null.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/reader\", \"TestReadSpacesOnlyValueIsNull\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC nulls-spaces-only ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The null set is exactly empty, `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL`, case-insensitive; no other token is null.\n- Null classification runs on trimmed values.\n- Null values are never listed in frequency output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-NumericTypes.md ===\n# FEATURE: Numeric Types\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Infer integer and number column types, testing integer before number. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-NullValues.md |\n| Provides    | InferType integer, number |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nPackage `internal/infer` exports `Infer(values []string) string`, where `values` are the trimmed\nvalues of one column. A column's type is the first matching type, in order, that every non-null\nvalue satisfies. This story implements `integer` and `number`; later stories add `date`, `boolean`,\n`text`, and `empty` to the same function. Until they land, a column matching neither numeric type\nreturns `text`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `integer`: every non-null value matches `-?[0-9]+`.\n- `number`: every non-null value matches `-?[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?`.\n- `integer` is tested before `number`, so a column of whole numbers is `integer` and one containing any decimal value is `number`.\n- Null values (per `IsNull`) are skipped.\n- No leading `+`, no exponent, no `1.`, no `.5`, no thousands separator, and no surrounding text match either type.\n- A column mixing numeric and non-numeric values does not match.\n- Examples from the supplied data: a column of ids is integer, a column of prices with two decimals is number, a column containing `A7` and `12.5` is not numeric.\n\n## Required Go tests\n\nWritten in `internal/infer`:\n\n| Test | Proves |\n|---|---|\n| `TestInferInteger` | whole numbers including negatives are integer |\n| `TestInferNumberWhenAnyDecimal` | one decimal value among whole numbers makes number |\n| `TestIntegerWinsOverNumber` | a column satisfying both is integer |\n| `TestInferSkipsNulls` | null values do not prevent a numeric type |\n| `TestInferRejectsMalformedNumbers` | `1.`, `.5`, `+1`, `1e3`, `1,5`, and `12abc` are neither integer nor number |\n| `TestInferMixedIsNotNumeric` | a column with a numeric and a non-numeric value is neither type |\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC numeric-integer ===\nIntent: Columns of whole numbers, negatives included, are typed integer.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/infer\", \"TestInferInteger\")\nprint(result.status)\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC numeric-integer ===\n\n=== AC numeric-number ===\nIntent: A column with any decimal value is typed number.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go;
